<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، يُسْأَلُ عَنْ قَضَاءِ، رَمَضَانَ فَقَالَ سَعِيدٌ أَحَبُّ إِلَىَّ أَنْ لاَ يُفَرَّقَ قَضَاءُ رَمَضَانَ وَأَنْ يُوَاتَرَ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ فِيمَنْ فَرَّقَ قَضَاءَ رَمَضَانَ فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ إِعَادَةٌ وَذَلِكَ مُجْزِئٌ عَنْهُ وَأَحَبُّ ذَلِكَ إِلَىَّ أَنْ يُتَابِعَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ مَنْ أَكَلَ أَوْ شَرِبَ فِي رَمَضَانَ سَاهِيًا أَوْ نَاسِيًا أَوْ مَا كَانَ مِنْ صِيَامٍ وَاجِبٍ عَلَيْهِ أَنَّ عَلَيْهِ قَضَاءَ يَوْمٍ مَكَانَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/678" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that he heard Said ibn al Musayyab being asked about making up days missed in Ramadan, and Said said, &quot;What I like best is for days missed in Ramadan to be made up consecutively, and not separately.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 678]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/678" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 678</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   678 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he had heard Malik say, about some one who made up the days he had missed in Ramadan separately, that he did not have to repeat them. (What he had done) was enough for him. It was, however, preferable, if he did them consecutively. Malik said, &quot;Whoever eats or drinks thoughtlessly or forgetfully in Ramadan or during any other obligatory fast that he must do, has to fast another day in its place</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/678" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">Yahya bin Saeed ne suna Saeed bin Musayyib se, pucha gaya unse Ramzan ki qaza ke baare mein to kaha Saeed ne, mere nazdeek yeh baat achi hai ke Ramzan ki qaza pai dar pai rakhe.</p>

## Narration 677

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 679</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   679 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ قَيْسٍ الْمَكِّيِّ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ قَالَ كُنْتُ مَعَ مُجَاهِدٍ وَهُوَ يَطُوفُ بِالْبَيْتِ فَجَاءَهُ إِنْسَانٌ فَسَأَلَهُ عَنْ صِيَامِ أَيَّامِ الْكَفَّارَةِ أَمُتَتَابِعَاتٍ أَمْ يَقْطَعُهَا قَالَ حُمَيْدٌ فَقُلْتُ لَهُ نَعَمْ يَقْطَعُهَا إِنْ شَاءَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مُجَاهِدٌ لاَ يَقْطَعُهَا فَإِنَّهَا فِي قِرَاءَةِ أُبَىِّ بْنِ كَعْبٍ ثَلاَثَةِ أَيَّامٍ مُتَتَابِعَاتٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَأَحَبُّ إِلَىَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ مَا سَمَّى اللَّهُ فِي الْقُرْآنِ يُصَامُ مُتَتَابِعًا ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنِ الْمَرْأَةِ تُصْبِحُ صَائِمَةً فِي رَمَضَانَ فَتَدْفَعُ دَفْعَةً مِنْ دَمٍ عَبِيطٍ فِي غَيْرِ أَوَانِ حَيْضِهَا ثُمَّ تَنْتَظِرُ حَتَّى تُمْسِيَ أَنْ تَرَى مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ فَلاَ تَرَى شَيْئًا ثُمَّ تُصْبِحُ يَوْمًا آخَرَ فَتَدْفَعُ دَفْعَةً أُخْرَى وَهِيَ دُونَ الأُولَى ثُمَّ يَنْقَطِعُ ذَلِكَ عَنْهَا قَبْلَ حَيْضَتِهَا بِأَيَّامٍ فَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ كَيْفَ تَصْنَعُ فِي صِيَامِهَا وَصَلاَتِهَا قَالَ مَالِكٌ ذَلِكَ الدَّمُ مِنَ الْحَيْضَةِ فَإِذَا رَأَتْهُ فَلْتُفْطِرْ وَلْتَقْضِ مَا أَفْطَرَتْ فَإِذَا ذَهَبَ عَنْهَا الدَّمُ فَلْتَغْتَسِلْ وَتَصُومُ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ عَمَّنْ أَسْلَمَ فِي آخِرِ يَوْمٍ مِنْ رَمَضَانَ هَلْ عَلَيْهِ قَضَاءُ رَمَضَانَ كُلِّهِ أَوْ يَجِبُ عَلَيْهِ قَضَاءُ الْيَوْمِ الَّذِي أَسْلَمَ فِيهِ فَقَالَ لَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ قَضَاءُ مَا مَضَى وَإِنَّمَا يَسْتَأْنِفُ الصِّيَامَ فِيمَا يُسْتَقْبَلُ وَأَحَبُّ إِلَىَّ أَنْ يَقْضِيَ الْيَوْمَ الَّذِي أَسْلَمَ فِيهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/679" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Mujahid (successor); Humayd ibn Oays al-Makki (unresolved). The source-attributed dialogue and Mujahid’s explicit citation of Ubayy’s recitation are retained. Following compiler rules and separate legal questions remain context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Humayd ibn Oays al-Makki told him, &quot;I was with Mujahid while he was performing tawaf around the Kaba, and a man came to him and asked whether the days (of fasting) for kaffara had to be fasted consecutively, or could they be split up. I said to him, &#x27;Yes, they can be split up, if the person so wishes.&#x27; Mujahid said, &#x27;He should not split them up, because in Ubayy ibn Kab&#x27;s recitation they are referred to as three consecutive days.&#x27; &quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 679]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/679" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 679</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   679 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;What I like most is what Allah has specified in the Qur&#x27;an, that is, that they are fasted consecutively.&quot; Malik was asked about a woman who began the day fasting in Ramadan and though it was outside of the time of her period, fresh blood (i.e. not menstrual blood) flowed from her. She then waited until evening to see the same, but did not see anything.Then, on the next day in the morning she had anotherflow, though less than the first. Then, some days before her period, the flow stopped completely. Malik was asked what she should do about her fasting and prayer, and he said, &quot;This blood is like menstrual blood. When she sees it she should break her fast, and then make up the days she has missed. Then, when the blood has completely stopped, she should do ghusl and fast.&quot; Malik was asked whether someone who became muslim on the last day of Ramadan had to make up all of Ramadan or whether he just had to make up the day when he became muslim, and he said, &quot;He does not have to make up any of the days that have passed. He begins fasting from that day onwards. What I like most is that he makes up the day on which he became muslim</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/679" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   679 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">Humaid bin Qais Makki se riwayat hai ke main saath tha Mujahid ke aur tawaf kar rahe the Khan-e-Kaaba ka, itne mein ek aadmi aaya aur pucha ke qasam ke kaffare ke roze pai dar pai hain ya juda juda? Humaid ne kaha haan, juda juda bhi rakh sakta hai agar chahe. Mujahid ne kaha nahi, kyunki Ubayy bin Ka&#x27;ab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ki qirat mein hai &#x27;thalathatu ayyamin mutatabi&#x27;at&#x27; yani roze teen din ke pai dar pai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/679" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حمید بن قیس مکی سے روایت ہے کہ میں ساتھ تھا مجاہد کے اور طواف کر رہے تھے خانہ کعبہ کا اتنے میں ایک آدمی آیا اور پوچھا کہ قسم کے کفارے کے روزے پے درپے ہیں یا جدا جدا حمید نے کہا ہاں جدا جدا بھی رکھ سکتا ہے اگر چاہے مجاہد نے کہا نہیں کیونکہ ابی بن کعب کی قرأت میں ہے ثلثة ایام متتابعات یعنی روزے تین دن کے پے درپے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Humaid bin Qais Makki se riwayat hai ke main saath tha Mujahid ke aur tawaf kar rahe the Khan-e-Kaaba ka, itne mein ek aadmi aaya aur pucha ke qasam ke kaffare ke roze pai dar pai hain ya juda juda? Humaid ne kaha haan, juda juda bhi rakh sakta hai agar chahe. Mujahid ne kaha nahi, kyunki Ubayy bin Ka&#x27;ab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ki qirat mein hai &#x27;thalathatu ayyamin mutatabi&#x27;at&#x27; yani roze teen din ke pai dar pai.</p>

## Narration 678

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A680)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ، وَحَفْصَةَ، زَوْجَىِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَصْبَحَتَا صَائِمَتَيْنِ مُتَطَوِّعَتَيْنِ فَأُهْدِيَ لَهُمَا طَعَامٌ فَأَفْطَرَتَا عَلَيْهِ فَدَخَلَ عَلَيْهِمَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ فَقَالَتْ حَفْصَةُ وَبَدَرَتْنِي بِالْكَلاَمِ - وَكَانَتْ بِنْتَ أَبِيهَا - يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنِّي أَصْبَحْتُ أَنَا وَعَائِشَةُ صَائِمَتَيْنِ مُتَطَوِّعَتَيْنِ فَأُهْدِيَ إِلَيْنَا طَعَامٌ فَأَفْطَرْنَا عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ اقْضِيَا مَكَانَهُ يَوْمًا آخَرَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 680]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/680" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that A&#x27;isha and Hafsa, the wives of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, began fasting voluntarily one morning and then food was given to them and they broke their fast with it. Then the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, came in. A&#x27;isha said, &quot;Hafsa asked, anticipating me in speech - she took after her father Umar - &#x27;Messenger of Allah, A&#x27;isha and I began the morning fasting voluntarily and then food was given us and we broke the fast with it.&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Fast another day in its place</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 680]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/680" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Ibn Shihab se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umm-ul-Momineen Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) aur Umm-ul-Momineen Hafsa (Radi Allahu Anha) subah uthin nafl roza rakh kar, phir khane ka hissa aaya to unhone roza khol dala. Itne mein Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) tashreef laye. Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) farmati hain ke Hafsa (Radi Allahu Anha) ne kaha (shuru kar diya mujhe bolne na diya, aakhir apne baap ki beti thin), Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), main aur Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) subah ko uthin nafl roza rakh kar to hamare paas hissa aaya khane ka, hum ne roza khol dala. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, iske ewaz (badle) mein ek roza qaza ka rakho.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 680]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/680" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت ام المومنین عائشہ اور ام المومنین حفصہ صبح اٹھیں نفل روزہ رکھ کر پھر کھانے کا حصہ آیا تو انہوں نے روزہ کھول ڈالا اتنے میں رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم تشریف لائے عائشہ فرماتی ہیں کہ حفصہ نے کہا شروع کر دیا مجھے بولنے نہ دیا آخر اپنے باپ کی بیٹی تھیں یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم میں اور عائشہ صبح کو اٹھیں نفل روزہ رکھ کر تو ہمارے پاس حصہ آیا کھانے کا ہم نے روزہ کھول ڈالا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اس کے عوض میں ایک روزہ قضا کا رکھو ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 680]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/680" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 679

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A681)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 681]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 681</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   681 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ أَنَسَ بْنَ مَالِكٍ، كَبِرَ حَتَّى كَانَ لاَ يَقْدِرُ عَلَى الصِّيَامِ فَكَانَ يَفْتَدِي ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ أَرَى ذَلِكَ وَاجِبًا وَأَحَبُّ إِلَىَّ أَنْ يَفْعَلَهُ إِذَا كَانَ قَوِيًّا عَلَيْهِ فَمَنْ فَدَى فَإِنَّمَا يُطْعِمُ مَكَانَ كُلِّ يَوْمٍ مُدًّا بِمُدِّ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Anas ibn Malik (companion). Anas’s separately transmitted practice is selected from between long compiler discussions; those discussions remain complete notes.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Anas ibn Malik used to pay fidya when he had grown old and could no longer manage to do the fast.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 681]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 681</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   681 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;Someone who eats or drinks out of neglect or forgetfulness during a voluntary fast does not have to repeat his fast, but he should continue fasting for the rest of the day in which he eats or drinks while voluntarily fasting, and not stop fasting. Someone to whom something unexpected happens which causes him to break his fast while he is fasting voluntarily does not have to repeat his fast if he has broken it for a reason, and not simply because he decided to break his fast. Just as I do not think that someone has to repeat a voluntary prayer if he has had to stop it because of some discharge which he could prevent and which meant that he had to repeat his wudu.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Once a man has begun doing any of the right actions (al-amal as-saliha) such as the prayer, the fast and the hajj, or similar right actions of a voluntary nature, he should not stop until he has completed it according to what the sunna for that action is. If he says the takbir he should not stop until he has prayed two rakas. If he is fasting he should not break his fast until he has completed that day&#x27;s fast. If he goes into ihram he should not return until he has completed his hajj, and if he begins doing tawaf he should not stop doing so until he has gone around the Kaba seven times. He should not stop doing any of these actions once he has started them until he has completed them, except if something happens such as illness or some other matter by which a man is excused. This is because Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;And eat and drink until the white thread becomes clear to you from the black thread of dawn, (and) then complete the fast until night-time,&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 187), and so he must complete his fast as Allah has said. Allah, the Exalted, (also) says, &#x27;And complete the hajj and the umra for Allah,&#x27; and so if a man were to go into ihram for a voluntary hajj having done his one obligatory hajj (on a previous occasion), he could not then stop doing his hajj having once begun it and leave ihram while in the middle of his hajj. Anyone that begins a voluntary act must complete it once he has begun doing it, just as an obligatory act must be completed . This is the best of what I have heard.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;I do not consider that to do so is obligatory, but what I like most is that a man does the fast when he is strong enough. Whoever pays compensation gives one mudd of food in place of every day, using the mudd of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 681]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 681</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   681 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">Yahya ne kaha ke unhon ne Imam Malik ko yeh kehte suna, &#x27;Jis ne nafl rozay ke dauran bhool kar kha ya pee liya, use apna roza dohrane ki zaroorat nahi hai, balkay use baqi din apna roza jaari rakhna chahiye. Agar koi ghair iradi taur par roza tod de to use roza dohrane ki zaroorat nahi.&#x27; Malik ne kaha, &#x27;Ek baar jab koi nek kaam shuru kar de, jaise namaz, roza ya hajj, to use tab tak nahi chhodna chahiye jab tak woh mukammal na ho jaye. Jis tarah kisi ne agar nafl namaz shuru ki to use do rakatein mukammal karni chahiyein. Agar woh rozay se hai to use din ka roza mukammal karna chahiye. Agar woh ahram baandh leta hai to hajj mukammal kiye baghair wapas na aaye, aur agar tawaf shuru kare to Kaba ke saat chakkar mukammal kare.&#x27; Yahya ne mujh se riwayat kiya ke Imam Malik ne suna ke Hazrat Anas ibn Malik Razi Allahu Anhu jab budhay ho jate the aur roza rakhne ki taqat na rehti thi to fidya dete the. Malik ne kaha, &#x27;Main ise wajib nahi samajhta, lekin mujhe yeh pasand hai ke aadmi roza rakhe jab woh taqat rakhta ho. Jo shakhs fidya de woh har din ke badle ek mudd (khajoor ya anaaj) de, Nabi Kareem Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ke mudd ke hisaab se.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 681]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 681</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   681 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے کہا کہ انہوں نے امام مالک کو یہ کہتے سنا، &#x27;جس نے نفل روزے کے دوران بھول کر کھا یا پی لیا، اسے اپنا روزہ دہرانے کی ضرورت نہیں ہے، بلکہ اسے باقی دن اپنا روزہ جاری رکھنا چاہیے۔ اگر کوئی غیر ارادی طور پر روزہ توڑ دے تو اسے روزہ دہرانے کی ضرورت نہیں۔&#x27; مالک نے کہا، &#x27;ایک بار جب کوئی نیک کام شروع کر دے، جیسے نماز، روزہ یا حج، تو اسے تب تک نہیں چھوڑنا چاہیے جب تک وہ مکمل نہ ہو جائے۔ جس طرح کسی نے اگر نفل نماز شروع کی تو اسے دو رکعتیں مکمل کرنی چاہئیں۔ اگر وہ روزے سے ہے تو اسے دن کا روزہ مکمل کرنا چاہیے۔ اگر وہ احرام باندھ لیتا ہے تو حج مکمل کیے بغیر واپس نہ آئے، اور اگر طواف شروع کرے تو کعبہ کے سات چکر مکمل کرے۔&#x27; یحییٰ نے مجھ سے روایت کیا کہ امام مالک نے سنا کہ حضرت انس بن مالک رضی اللہ عنہ جب بوڑھے ہو جاتے تھے اور روزہ رکھنے کی طاقت نہ رہتی تھی تو فدیہ دیتے تھے۔ مالک نے کہا، &#x27;میں اسے واجب نہیں سمجھتا، لیکن مجھے یہ پسند ہے کہ آدمی روزہ رکھے جب وہ طاقت رکھتا ہو۔ جو شخص فدیہ دے وہ ہر دن کے بدلے ایک مد (کھجور یا اناج) دے، نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے مد کے حساب سے۔&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/681" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 680

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A682)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 682]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/682" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 682</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   682 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، سُئِلَ عَنِ الْمَرْأَةِ الْحَامِلِ، إِذَا خَافَتْ عَلَى وَلَدِهَا وَاشْتَدَّ عَلَيْهَا الصِّيَامُ قَالَ تُفْطِرُ وَتُطْعِمُ مَكَانَ كُلِّ يَوْمٍ مِسْكِينًا مُدًّا مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ بِمُدِّ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَأَهْلُ الْعِلْمِ يَرَوْنَ عَلَيْهَا الْقَضَاءَ كَمَا قَالَ اللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ ‏{‏فَمَنْ كَانَ مِنْكُمْ مَرِيضًا أَوْ عَلَى سَفَرٍ فَعِدَّةٌ مِنْ أَيَّامٍ أُخَرَ‏}‏ وَيَرَوْنَ ذَلِكَ مَرَضًا مِنَ الأَمْرَاضِ مَعَ الْخَوْفِ عَلَى وَلَدِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/682" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Abdullah ibn Umar was asked about what a pregnant woman should do if the fast became difficult for her and she feared for her child, and he said, &quot;She should break the fast and feed a poor man one mudd of wheat in place of every day, using the mudd of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 682]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/682" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 682</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   682 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The people of knowledge consider that she has to make up for each day of the fast that she misses as Allah, the Exalted and Glorified, says, &#x27;And whoever of you is sick or on a journey should fast an equal number of other days, &#x27; and they consider her pregnancy and her concern for her child as a sickness</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/682" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

</section>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक को पहुंचा के अनस बिन मालिक (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) बूढ़े हो गए थे यहाँ तक के रोज़ा न रख सकते थे तो फिडिया देते थे. इमाम मालिक को पहुंचा के अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से सवाल हुआ कि हामिला औरत अगर खौफ करे अपने हमल का और रोज़ा न रख सके तो कहा उन्होंने रोज़ा न रखे और हर रोज़े के बदले में एक मिस्कीन को एक मुद्द गेहू दे रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के मदद से.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/682" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ko pahuncha ke Anas bin Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) burhe ho gaye the yahan tak ke roza na rakh sakte the to fidiya dete the. Imam Malik ko pahuncha ke Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se sawal hua ke hamila aurat agar khauf kare apne hamal ka aur roza na rakh sake to kaha unhone roza na rakhe aur har roze ke badle mein ek miskeen ko ek mudd gehu de Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke mudd se.</p>

## Narration 681

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ مَنْ كَانَ عَلَيْهِ قَضَاءُ رَمَضَانَ فَلَمْ يَقْضِهِ - وَهُوَ قَوِيٌّ عَلَى صِيَامِهِ - حَتَّى جَاءَ رَمَضَانُ آخَرُ فَإِنَّهُ يُطْعِمُ مَكَانَ كُلِّ يَوْمٍ مِسْكِينًا مُدًّا مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ وَعَلَيْهِ مَعَ ذَلِكَ الْقَضَاءُ ‏.‏ وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، مِثْلُ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/683" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The father’s direct saying and the separate abbreviated report from Said ibn Jubayr remain distinct. The father’s unstated full name and the abbreviated wording are not reconstructed. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Said ibn Jubayr (successor). The father’s direct saying and the separate abbreviated report from Said ibn Jubayr remain distinct. The father’s unstated full name and the abbreviated wording are not reconstructed.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Qasim that his father used to say, &quot;If someone has to make up for days not fasted in Ramadan and does not do them before the next Ramadan comes although he is strong enough to do so, he should feed a poor man with a mudd of wheat for every day that he has missed, and he has to fast the days he owes as well.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard the same thing from Said ibn Jubayr</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 683]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/683" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद से रिवायत है वो कहते थे जिस शख्स पर रमजान की क़ज़ा लाज़िम हो फिर वो क़ज़ा न करे यहाँ तक के दूसरा रमजान आ जाये और वो क़ादिर रहा हो रोज़े पर तो हर रोज़े के बदले में एक-एक मिस्कीन को एक-एक मुद्द गेहू का दे और क़ज़ा भी रखे. इमाम मालिक को सईद बिन जुबैर से भी ऐसा ही पहुंचा.</p>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد سے روایت ہے وہ کہتے تھے جس شخص پر رمضان کی قضا لازم ہو پھر وہ قضا نہ کرے یہاں تک کہ دوسرا رمضان آجائے اور وہ قادر رہا ہو روزے پر تو ہر روزے کے بدلے میں ایک ایک مسکین کو ایک ایک مد گیہوں کا دے اور قضا بھی رکھے ۔ امام مالک کو سعید بن جبیر سے بھی ایسا ہی پہنچا</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم تَقُولُ إِنْ كَانَ لَيَكُونُ عَلَىَّ الصِّيَامُ مِنْ رَمَضَانَ فَمَا أَسْتَطِيعُ أَصُومُهُ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَ شَعْبَانُ</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman that he heard A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say, &quot;I used to have to make up days from Ramadan and not be able to do them until Shaban came</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 685]</p>

## Narration 683

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A686)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَصُومُ حَتَّى نَقُولَ لاَ يُفْطِرُ وَيُفْطِرُ حَتَّى نَقُولَ لاَ يَصُومُ وَمَا رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم اسْتَكْمَلَ صِيَامَ شَهْرٍ قَطُّ إِلاَّ رَمَضَانَ وَمَا رَأَيْتُهُ فِي شَهْرٍ أَكْثَرَ صِيَامًا مِنْهُ فِي شَعْبَانَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 686]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/686" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   686 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard the people of knowledge telling people not to fast on the day in Shaban when there was doubt (about whether it was Shaban or Ramadan), if they intended by it the fast of Ramadan . They considered that whoever fasted on that day without having seen (the new moon) had to make up that day if it later became clear that it was part of Ramadan. They did not see any harm in voluntary fasting on that day. Malik said, &quot;This is what we do, and what I have seen the people of knowledge in our city doing.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;n Nadr, the mawla of Umar ibn Ubaydullah, from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to fast for so long that we thought he would never stop fasting, and he would go without fasting for so long that we thought he would never fast again. I never saw the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, fast for a complete month except for Ramadan, and I never saw him do more fasting in any one month than he did in Shaban</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 686]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/686" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मे मोमिनीन आयेशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) रोज़े रखते थे यहाँ तक कि हम कहते थे अब इफ्तार न करेंगे और फिर इफ्तार करते थे यहाँ तक कि हम कहते थे अब रोज़ा न रखेंगे और मैं ने नहीं देखा रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को के किसी महीने के पूरे रोज़े रखे हैं सिवाए रमजान के और किसी महीने में शबन से ज़्यादा रोज़े न रखते थे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 686]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/686" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المومنین عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم روزے رکھتے تھے یہاں تک کہ ہم کہتے تھے اب افطار نہ کریں گے اور پھر افطار کرتے تھے یہاں تک کہ ہم کہتے تھے اب روزہ نہ رکھیں گے اور میں نے نہیں دیکھا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو کہ کسی مہینہ کے پورے روزہ رکھے ہوں سوائے رمضان کے اور کسی مہینے میں شعبان سے زیادہ روزے نہ رکھتے تھے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 686]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/686" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Umme Momineen Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) roze rakhte the yahan tak ke hum kehte the ab iftar na karenge aur phir iftar karte the yahan tak ke hum kehte the ab roza na rakhenge aur main ne nahi dekha Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko ke kisi mahine ke poore roze rakhe hon siwaye Ramzan ke aur kisi mahine mein Shaban se zyada roze na rakhte the.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 686]</p>

## Narration 684

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A687)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الصِّيَامُ جُنَّةٌ فَإِذَا كَانَ أَحَدُكُمْ صَائِمًا فَلاَ يَرْفُثْ وَلاَ يَجْهَلْ فَإِنِ امْرُؤٌ قَاتَلَهُ أَوْ شَاتَمَهُ فَلْيَقُلْ إِنِّي صَائِمٌ إِنِّي صَائِمٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 687]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/687" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z Zinad from al-A&#x27;raj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Fasting is a protection for you, so when you are fasting, do not behave obscenely or foolishly, and if any one argues with you or abuses you, say, &#x27;I am fasting. I am fasting</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 687]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/687" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया रोज़ा ढाल है, तो जब तुम में से कोई रोज़ादार हो तो चाहिए के बेहूदा न बके और जहालत न करे, अगर कोई शख्स उसे गालियां बके या लड़े तो कह दे मैं रोज़ादार हूँ, मैं रोज़ादार हूँ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 687]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/687" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا روزہ ڈھال ہے تو جب تم میں سے کوئی روزہ دار ہو تو چاہیے کہ بے ہودہ نہ بکے اور جہالت نہ کرے اگر کوئی شخص اسے گالیاں بکے یا لڑے تو کہہ دے میں روزہ دار ہوں میں روزہ دار ہوں ۔</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya roza dhaal hai, to jab tum mein se koi rozadar ho to chahiye ke behuda na bake aur jahalat na kare, agar koi shakhs use galiyan bake ya lade to keh de main rozadar hoon, main rozadar hoon.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 687]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/687" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 685

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A688)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 18</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ وَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لَخُلُوفُ فَمِ الصَّائِمِ أَطْيَبُ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ مِنْ رِيحِ الْمِسْكِ إِنَّمَا يَذَرُ شَهْوَتَهُ وَطَعَامَهُ وَشَرَابَهُ مِنْ أَجْلِي فَالصِّيَامُ لِي وَأَنَا أَجْزِي بِهِ كُلُّ حَسَنَةٍ بِعَشْرِ أَمْثَالِهَا إِلَى سَبْعِمِائَةِ ضِعْفٍ إِلاَّ الصِّيَامَ فَهُوَ لِي وَأَنَا أَجْزِي بِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 688]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/688" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 688</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   688 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z Zinad from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;By the One in Whose hand my self is, the smell of the breath of a man fasting is better with Allah than the scent of musk.&#x27; He leaves his desires and his food and drink for My sake. Fasting is for Me and I reward it. Every good action is rewarded by ten times its kind, up to seven hundred times, except fasting, which is for Me, and I reward it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 688]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/688" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   688 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया कसम है उस ज़ात की जिसके कब्ज़े में मेरी जान है, अलबत्ता रोज़ादार के मुँह की बू ज़्यादा पसंद है मुश्क की बू से अल्लाह जल्ल जलालुहू के नज़दीक, क्यूंके वो छोड़ देता है अपनी ख्वाहिशों को और खाने को और पानी को मेरे वास्ते, तो वो रोज़ा मेरे वास्ते है और मैं इसका बदला दूंगा, जो नेकी है उसका सवाब दस गुने से लेकर सात सौ गुने तक मिलेगा, मगर रोज़ा वो मेरे वास्ते है और उसका सवाब भी मैं ही दूंगा।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 688]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/688" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا قسم ہے اس ذات کی جس کے قبضے میں میری جان ہے البتہ روزہ دار کے منہ کی بو زیادہ پسند ہے مشک کی بو سے اللہ جل جلالہ کے نزدیک کیونکہ وہ چھوڑ دیتا ہے اپنی خواہشوں کو اور کھانے کو اور پانی کو میرے واسطے تو وہ روزہ میرے واسطے ہے اور میں اس کا بدلہ دوں گا جو نیکی ہے اس کا ثواب دس گنے سے لے کر سات سو گنے تک ملے گا مگر روزہ وہ میرے واسطے ہے اور اس کا ثواب بھی میں ہی دوں گا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 688]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/688" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya qasam hai us zaat ki jiske qabze mein meri jaan hai, albata rozadar ke munh ki boo zyada pasand hai mushk ki boo se Allah Jalla Jalaluhu ke nazdeek, kyunke wo chhod deta hai apni khwahishon ko aur khane ko aur pani ko mere waste, to wo roza mere waste hai aur main iska badla dunga, jo neki hai uska sawab das gune se lekar saat sau gune tak milega, magar roza wo mere waste hai aur uska sawab bhi main hi dunga.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 688]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/688" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَمِّهِ أَبِي سُهَيْلِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ إِذَا دَخَلَ رَمَضَانُ فُتِّحَتْ أَبْوَابُ الْجَنَّةِ وَغُلِّقَتْ أَبْوَابُ النَّارِ وَصُفِّدَتِ الشَّيَاطِينُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/689" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Hurayra (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from his paternal uncle Abu Suhayl ibn Malik from his father that Abu Hurayra said, &quot;When Ramadan comes the gates of the Garden are opened and the gates of the Fire are locked, and the shayatin are chained</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 689]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/689" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 687

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A690)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا اعْتَكَفَ يُدْنِي إِلَىَّ رَأْسَهُ فَأُرَجِّلُهُ وَكَانَ لاَ يَدْخُلُ الْبَيْتَ إِلاَّ لِحَاجَةِ الإِنْسَانِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 690]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/690" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 690</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   690 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that the people of knowledge did not disapprove of people fasting using tooth-sticks at any hour of the day in Ramadan, whether at the beginning or the end, nor had he heard any of the people of knowledge disapproving of or forbidding the practice. Yahya said that he heard Malik say, about fasting for six days after breaking the fast at the end of Ramadan, that he had never seen any of the people of knowledge and fiqh fasting them. He said, &quot;I have not heard that any of our predecessors used to do that, and the people of knowledge disapprove of it and they are afraid that it might become a bida and that common and ignorant people might join to Ramadan what does not belong to it, if they were to think that the people of knowledge had given permission for that to be done and were seen doing it. Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;I have never heard any of the people of knowledge and fiqh and those whom people take as an example forbidding fasting on the day of jumua. Fasting on it is good, and I have seen one of the people of knowledge fasting it, and it seemed to me that he was keen to do so.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr from Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said,&quot;When the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, did itikaf he would bring his head near to me and I would comb it. He would only go into the house to relieve himself</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 690]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/690" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत उम्मे मोमिनीन आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जब एतिकाफ़ में होते तो झुका देते सर अपना मेरी तरफ, सो मैं कंघी कर देती और घर में न आते मगर हाजत-ए-ज़रूरी के वास्ते।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 690]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/690" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ام المومنین عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم جب اعتکاف میں ہوتے تو جھکا دیتے سر اپنا میری طرف سو میں کنگھی کر دیتی اور گھر میں نہ آتے مگر حاجت ضروری کے واسطے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 690]</p>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Umme Momineen Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jab aitikaf mein hote to jhuka dete sar apna meri taraf, so main kanghi kar deti aur ghar mein na aate magar haajat-e-zaroori ke waste.</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ، كَانَتْ إِذَا اعْتَكَفَتْ لاَ تَسْأَلُ عَنِ الْمَرِيضِ إِلاَّ وَهِيَ تَمْشِي لاَ تَقِفُ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that when A&#x27;isha was doing itikaf she would only ask after sick people if she was walking and not if she was standing still.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 691]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/691" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 691</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   691 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;A person doing itikaf should not carry out obligations of his, nor leave the mosque for them, nor should he help anyone. He should only leave the mosque to relieve himself. If he were able to go out to do things for people, visiting the sick, praying over the dead and following funeral processions would be the things with the most claim on his coming out.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A person doing itikaf is not doing itikaf until he avoids what some one doing itikaf should avoid, namely, visiting the sick, praying over the dead, and entering houses, except to relieve himself</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/691" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

## Narration 689

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A692)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ، يَعْتَكِفُ هَلْ يَدْخُلُ لِحَاجَتِهِ تَحْتَ سَقْفٍ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ لاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ أَنَّهُ لاَ يُكْرَهُ الاِعْتِكَافُ فِي كُلِّ مَسْجِدٍ يُجَمَّعُ فِيهِ وَلاَ أُرَاهُ كُرِهَ الاِعْتِكَافُ فِي الْمَسَاجِدِ الَّتِي لاَ يُجَمَّعُ فِيهَا إِلاَّ كَرَاهِيَةَ أَنْ يَخْرُجَ الْمُعْتَكِفُ مِنْ مَسْجِدِهِ الَّذِي اعْتَكَفَ فِيهِ إِلَى الْجُمُعَةِ أَوْ يَدَعَهَا فَإِنْ كَانَ مَسْجِدًا لاَ يُجَمَّعُ فِيهِ الْجُمُعَةُ وَلاَ يَجِبُ عَلَى صَاحِبِهِ إِتْيَانُ الْجُمُعَةِ فِي مَسْجِدٍ سِوَاهُ فَإِنِّي لاَ أَرَى بَأْسًا بِالاِعْتِكَافِ فِيهِ لأَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى قَالَ ‏{‏وَأَنْتُمْ عَاكِفُونَ فِي الْمَسَاجِدِ‏}‏ فَعَمَّ اللَّهُ الْمَسَاجِدَ كُلَّهَا وَلَمْ يَخُصَّ شَيْئًا مِنْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَمِنْ هُنَالِكَ جَازَ لَهُ أَنْ يَعْتَكِفَ فِي الْمَسَاجِدِ الَّتِي لاَ يُجَمَّعُ فِيهَا الْجُمُعَةُ إِذَا كَانَ لاَ يَجِبُ عَلَيْهِ أَنْ يَخْرُجَ مِنْهُ إِلَى الْمَسْجِدِ الَّذِي تُجَمَّعُ فِيهِ الْجُمُعَةُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ يَبِيتُ الْمُعْتَكِفُ إِلاَّ فِي الْمَسْجِدِ الَّذِي اعْتَكَفَ فِيهِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ خِبَاؤُهُ فِي رَحَبَةٍ مِنْ رِحَابِ الْمَسْجِدِ وَلَمْ أَسْمَعْ أَنَّ الْمُعْتَكِفَ يَضْرِبُ بِنَاءً يَبِيتُ فِيهِ إِلاَّ فِي الْمَسْجِدِ أَوْ فِي رَحَبَةٍ مِنْ رِحَابِ الْمَسْجِدِ وَمِمَّا يَدُلُّ عَلَى أَنَّهُ لاَ يَبِيتُ إِلاَّ فِي الْمَسْجِدِ قَوْلُ عَائِشَةَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا اعْتَكَفَ لاَ يَدْخُلُ الْبَيْتَ إِلاَّ لِحَاجَةِ الإِنْسَانِ ‏.‏ وَلاَ يَعْتَكِفُ فَوْقَ ظَهْرِ الْمَسْجِدِ وَلاَ فِي الْمَنَارِ يَعْنِي الصَّوْمَعَةَ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ مَالِكٌ يَدْخُلُ الْمُعْتَكِفُ الْمَكَانَ الَّذِي يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَعْتَكِفَ فِيهِ قَبْلَ غُرُوبِ الشَّمْسِ مِنَ اللَّيْلَةِ الَّتِي يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَعْتَكِفَ فِيهَا حَتَّى يَسْتَقْبِلَ بِاعْتِكَافِهِ أَوَّلَ اللَّيْلَةِ الَّتِي يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَعْتَكِفَ فِيهَا وَالْمُعْتَكِفُ مُشْتَغِلٌ بِاعْتِكَافِهِ لاَ يَعْرِضُ لِغَيْرِهِ مِمَّا يَشْتَغِلُ بِهِ مِنَ التِّجَارَاتِ أَوْ غَيْرِهَا وَلاَ بَأْسَ بِأَنْ يَأْمُرَ الْمُعْتَكِفُ بِبَعْضِ حَاجَتِهِ بِضَيْعَتِهِ وَمَصْلَحَةِ أَهْلِهِ وَأَنْ يَأْمُرَ بِبَيْعِ مَالِهِ أَوْ بِشَىْءٍ لاَ يَشْغَلُهُ فِي نَفْسِهِ فَلاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ إِذَا كَانَ خَفِيفًا أَنْ يَأْمُرَ بِذَلِكَ مَنْ يَكْفِيهِ إِيَّاهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لَمْ أَسْمَعْ أَحَدًا مِنْ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ يَذْكُرُ فِي الاِعْتِكَافِ شَرْطًا وَإِنَّمَا الاِعْتِكَافُ عَمَلٌ مِنَ الأَعْمَالِ مِثْلُ الصَّلاَةِ وَالصِّيَامِ وَالْحَجِّ وَمَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ الأَعْمَالِ مَا كَانَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَرِيضَةً أَوْ نَافِلَةً فَمَنْ دَخَلَ فِي شَىْءٍ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَإِنَّمَا يَعْمَلُ بِمَا مَضَى مِنَ السُّنَّةِ وَلَيْسَ لَهُ أَنْ يُحْدِثَ فِي ذَلِكَ غَيْرَ مَا مَضَى عَلَيْهِ الْمُسْلِمُونَ لاَ مِنْ شَرْطٍ يَشْتَرِطُهُ وَلاَ يَبْتَدِعُهُ وَقَدِ اعْتَكَفَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَعَرَفَ الْمُسْلِمُونَ سُنَّةَ الاِعْتِكَافِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالاِعْتِكَافَ وَالْجِوَارُ سَوَاءٌ وَالاِعْتِكَافُ لِلْقَرَوِيِّ وَالْبَدَوِيِّ سَوَاءٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 692]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/692" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had asked Ibn Shihab whether someone doing itikaf could go into a house to relieve himself, and he said, &quot;Yes, there is no harm in that.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The situation that we are all agreed upon here is that there is no disapproval of anyone doing itikaf in a mosque where jumua is held. The only reason I see for disapproving of doing itikaf in a mosque where jumua is not held is that the man doing itikaf would have to leave the mosque where he was doing itikaf in order to go to jumua, or else not go there at all. If, however, he is doing itikaf in a mosque where jumua is not held, and he does not have to go to jumua in any other mosque, then I see no harm in him doing itikaf there, because Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, says, &#x27;While you are doing itikaf in mosques,&#x27; and refers to all mosques in general, without specifying any particular kind.&quot; Malik continued, &quot;Accordingly, it is permissiblefor a man to do itikaf in a mosque where jumua is not held if he does not have to leave it to go to a mosque where jumua is held.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A person doing itikaf should spend the night only in the mosque where he is doing itikaf, except if his tent is in one of the courtyards of the mosque. I have never heard that someone doing itikaf can put up a shelter anywhere except in the mosque itself or in one of the courtyards of the mosque. Part of what shows that he must spend the night in the mosque is the saying of A&#x27;isha, &#x27;When the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was doing itikaf, he would only go into the house to relieve himself.&#x27; Nor should he do itikaf on the roof of the mosque or in the minaret.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The person who is going to do itikaf should enter the place where he wishes to do itikaf before the sun sets on the night when he wishes to begin his itikaf, so that he is ready to begin the itikaf at the beginning of the night when he is going to start his itikaf. A person doing itikaf should be occupied with his itikaf, and not turn his attention to other things which might occupy him, such as trading or whatever. There is no harm, however, if some one doing itikaf tells some one to do something for him regarding his estate, or the affairs of his family, or tells someone to sell some property of his, or something else that does not occupy him directly. There is no harm in him arranging for someone else to do that for him if it is a simple matter.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I have never heard any of the people of knowledge mentioning any modification as far as how to do itikaf is concerned. Itikaf is an act of ibada like the prayer, fasting, the hajj, and such like acts, whether they are obligatory or voluntary. Anyone who begins doing any of these acts should do them according to what has come down in the sunna. He should not start doing anything in them that the muslims have not done, whether it is a modification that he imposes on others, or one that he begins doing himself. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, practised itikaf, and the muslims know what the sunna of itikaf is.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Itikaf and jiwar are the same, and Itikaf is the same for a village-dweller as it is for a nomad</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 692]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/692" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक से रिवायत है के उन्होंने इब्न-इ-शिहाब से पूछा के मुताकिफ़ को पाते हुए मकान में हाजत-इ-ज़रूरी के लिए जाना दुरुस्त है? बोले हाँ दुरुस्त है</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 692]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/692" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے پوچھا ابن شہاب سے کہ معتکف کو پٹے ہوئے مکان میں حاجت ضروری کو جانا درست ہے بولے ہاں درست ہے کچھ حرج نہیں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 692]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik se riwayat hai ke unhone Ibn-e-Shihab se pucha ke mutakif ko pate hue makaan mein haajat-e-zaroori ke liye jana durust hai? Bole haan durust hai, kuch harj nahi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 692]</p>

## Narration 690

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   693 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ، وَنَافِعًا، مَوْلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ قَالاَ لاَ اعْتِكَافَ إِلاَّ بِصِيَامٍ بِقَوْلِ اللَّهِ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَكُلُوا وَاشْرَبُوا حَتَّى يَتَبَيَّنَ لَكُمُ الْخَيْطُ الأَبْيَضُ مِنَ الْخَيْطِ الأَسْوَدِ مِنَ الْفَجْرِ ثُمَّ أَتِمُّوا الصِّيَامَ إِلَى اللَّيْلِ وَلاَ تُبَاشِرُوهُنَّ وَأَنْتُمْ عَاكِفُونَ فِي الْمَسَاجِدِ‏}‏ فَإِنَّمَا ذَكَرَ اللَّهُ الاِعْتِكَافَ مَعَ الصِّيَامِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّهُ لاَ اعْتِكَافَ إِلاَّ بِصِيَامٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/693" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor); Nafi (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that al-Qasim ibn Muhammad and Nafi, the mawla of Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;You cannot do itikaf unless you are fasting, because of what Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;And eat and drink until the white thread becomes clear to you from the black thread of dawn, then complete the fast until night-time, and do not have intercourse with them while you are doing itikaf in mosques,&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 187). Allah only mentions itikaf together with fasting.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 693]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/693" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">कासिम बिन मुहम्मद और नाफ़े (जो मौला हैं अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर के) दोनों कहते थे के एतिकाफ़ बगेर रोज़े के दुरुस्त नहीं है क्यूंके अल्लाह जल्ल जलालुहू ने फरमाया अपनी किताब में: खाओ और पियो यहां तक के सफ़ेद धारी मालूम होने लगे स्याह धारी से फ़ज्र की, तमाम करो रोज़ों को रात तक और न चमाटको अपनी औरतों से जब तुम एतिकाफ़ से हो मस्जिदों में, तो ज़िक्र किया अल्लाह जल्ल जलालुहू ने एतिकाफ़ का रोज़े के साथ।</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد اور نافع جو مولیٰ ہیں عبداللہ بن عمر کے دونوں کہتے تھے کہ اعتکاف بغیر روزے کے درست نہیں ہے کیونکہ اللہ جل جلالہ نے فرمایا اپنی کتاب میں کھاؤ اور پیو یہاں تک کہ سفید دھاری معلوم ہونے لگے سیاہ دھاری سے فجر کی تمام کرو روزوں کو رات تک اور نہ چمٹو اپنی عورتوں سے جب تم اعتکاف سے ہو مسجدوں میں تو ذکر کیا اللہ جل جلالہ نے اعتکاف کا روزے کے ساتھ ۔</p>

<details>

<p dir="ltr">Qasim bin Muhammad aur Nafe (jo maula hain Abdullah bin Umar ke) dono kehte the ke aitikaf baghair rozey ke durust nahi hai kyunki Allah Jalla Jalalahu ne farmaya apni kitab mein: khao aur piyo yahan tak ke safed dhari maloom hone lage siyah dhari se fajr ki, tamam karo rozon ko raat tak aur na chamatko apni aurton se jab tum aitikaf se ho masjidon mein, to zikr kiya Allah Jalla Jalalahu ne aitikaf ka rozey ke sath.</p>

<p dir="auto">समय (जो मौला हैं आबि बक्र के) उन से रिवायत है के अबु बक्र बिन अब्दुर रहमान ऐतिकाफ करते तो जाते हाजत-इ-ज़रूरी के वास्ते एक छत दर कोठरी में जो बंद रहती खालिद बिन वलीद के घर में हाज़िर होते ईद में साथ मुसलमानों के.</p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: ابوبکر بن عبدالرحمن (successor). The Urdu source supplies this transmitted practice while Arabic and English are empty. Its text and attribution are preserved independently of the Bengali field.</p>

<p dir="rtl">سمی جو مولیٰ ہیں ابی بکر کے ان سے روایت ہے کہ ابوبکر بن عبدالرحمن اعتکاف کرتے تو جاتے حاجت ضروری کے واسطے ایک چھت دار کوٹھری میں جو بند رہتی خالد بن ولید کے گھر میں، پھر نہ نکلتے اعتکاف سے یہاں تک کہ حاضر ہوتے عید میں ساتھ مسلمانوں کے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 694]</p>

## Narration 692

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A695)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَرَادَ أَنْ يَعْتَكِفَ فَلَمَّا انْصَرَفَ إِلَى الْمَكَانِ الَّذِي أَرَادَ أَنْ يَعْتَكِفَ فِيهِ وَجَدَ أَخْبِيَةً خِبَاءَ عَائِشَةَ وَخِبَاءَ حَفْصَةَ وَخِبَاءَ زَيْنَبَ فَلَمَّا رَآهَا سَأَلَ عَنْهَا فَقِيلَ لَهُ هَذَا خِبَاءُ عَائِشَةَ وَحَفْصَةَ وَزَيْنَبَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ آلْبِرَّ تَقُولُونَ بِهِنَّ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ انْصَرَفَ فَلَمْ يَعْتَكِفْ حَتَّى اعْتَكَفَ عَشْرًا مِنْ شَوَّالٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 695]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/695" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उमरा बिंत अब्दुर रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इरादा किया एतिकाफ़ का। जब आए आप उस जगह जहां एतिकाफ़ करना चाहते थे, पाए आप ने कई खैमे; एक खैमा आयशा का, एक खैमा हफ़्सा का और एक खैमा ज़ैनब का। तो पूछा आप ने ये किन के खैमे हैं? लोगों ने कहा आयशा, हफ़्सा और ज़ैनब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुन्न) के। फरमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने, क्या तुम नेकी का गुमान करते हो इन औरतों के साथ? फिर लौट आए आप और एतिकाफ़ न किया, फिर शव्वाल के दस दिनों में एतिकाफ़ किया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 695]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/695" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عمرہ بنت عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ارادہ کیا اعتکاف کا جب آئے آپ اس جگہ جہاں اعتکاف کرنا چاہتے تھے پائے آپ نے کئی خیمے ایک خیمہ عائشہ کا اور ایک خیمہ حفصہ کا اور ایک خیمہ زینب کا تو پوچھا آپ نے یہ کن کے خیمے ہیں لوگوں نے کہا عائشہ اور حفصہ اور زینب رضی اللہ عنھن کے ۔ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے کیا تم نیکی کا گمان کرتے ہو ان عورتوں کے ساتھ، پھر لوٹ آئے آپ اور اعتکاف نہ کیا پھر شوال کے دس دنوں میں اعتکاف کیا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 695]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/695" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Umrah bint Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne irada kiya aitikaf ka. Jab aaye aap us jagah jahan aitikaf karna chahte the, paye aap ne kai khaime; ek khaima Aisha ka, ek khaima Hafsa ka aur ek khaima Zainab ka. To pucha aap ne ye kin ke khaime hain? Logon ne kaha Aisha, Hafsa aur Zainab (Radi Allahu Anhun) ke. Farmaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne, kya tum neki ka guman karte ho in aurton ke sath? Phir laut aaye aap aur aitikaf na kiya, phir Shawwal ke das dinon mein aitikaf kiya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 695]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/695" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 693

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A696)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، ‏.‏ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يَذْهَبُ لِحَاجَةِ الإِنْسَانِ فِي الْبُيُوتِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ يَخْرُجُ الْمُعْتَكِفُ مَعَ جَنَازَةِ أَبَوَيْهِ وَلاَ مَعَ غَيْرِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 696]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/696" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 696</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   696 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik was asked whether someone who went into a mosque to do itikaf for the last ten days of Ramadan and stayed there for a day or two but then became ill and left the mosque, had to do itikaf for the number of days that were left from the ten, or not, and if he did have to do so, then what month should he do it in, and he replied, &quot;He should make up whatever he has to do of the itikaf when he recovers, whether in Ramadan or otherwise. I have heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, once wanted to do itikaf in Ramadan, but then came back without having done so, and then when Ramadan had gone, he did itikaf for ten days in Shawwal. Some one who does itikaf voluntarily in Ramadan and some one who has to do itikaf are in the same position regarding what is halal for them and what is haram. I have not heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ever did itikaf other than voluntarily.&quot; Malik said, that if a woman did itikaf and then menstruated during her itikaf, she went back to her house, and, when she was pure again she returned to the mosque, at whatever time it was that she became pure. She then continued her itikaf from where she left off. This was the same situation as with a woman who had to fast two consecutive months, and who menstruated and then became pure. She then continued the fast from where she had left off and did not delay doing so. Ziyad related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to go to relieve himself in houses. Malik said, &quot;Someone doing itikaf should not leave for his parents&#x27; funeral or for anything else</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 696]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/696" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">इब्न-इ-शिहाब से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हाजत-इ-ज़रूरी के लिए घरों में आये थे ऐतिकाफ की हालत में.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 696]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/696" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 694

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A697)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْهَادِ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ التَّيْمِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَعْتَكِفُ الْعَشْرَ الْوُسُطَ مِنْ رَمَضَانَ فَاعْتَكَفَ عَامًا حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَ لَيْلَةَ إِحْدَى وَعِشْرِينَ وَهِيَ اللَّيْلَةُ الَّتِي يَخْرُجُ فِيهَا مِنْ صُبْحِهَا مِنَ اعْتِكَافِهِ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنِ اعْتَكَفَ مَعِي فَلْيَعْتَكِفِ الْعَشْرَ الأَوَاخِرَ وَقَدْ رَأَيْتُ هَذِهِ اللَّيْلَةَ ثُمَّ أُنْسِيتُهَا وَقَدْ رَأَيْتُنِي أَسْجُدُ مِنْ صُبْحِهَا فِي مَاءٍ وَطِينٍ فَالْتَمِسُوهَا فِي الْعَشْرِ الأَوَاخِرِ وَالْتَمِسُوهَا فِي كُلِّ وِتْرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو سَعِيدٍ فَأُمْطِرَتِ السَّمَاءُ تِلْكَ اللَّيْلَةَ وَكَانَ الْمَسْجِدُ عَلَى عَرِيشٍ فَوَكَفَ الْمَسْجِدُ - قَالَ أَبُو سَعِيدٍ - فَأَبْصَرَتْ عَيْنَاىَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم انْصَرَفَ وَعَلَى جَبْهَتِهِ وَأَنْفِهِ أَثَرُ الْمَاءِ وَالطِّينِ مِنْ صُبْحِ لَيْلَةِ إِحْدَى وَعِشْرِينَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 697]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/697" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in someone who is in itikaf entering into a marriage contract as long as there is no physical relationship. A woman in itikaf may also be betrothed as long as there is no physical relationship. What is haram for someone in itikaf in relation to his womenfolk during the day is haram for him during the night.&quot; Yahya said that Ziyad said that Malik said, &quot;It is not halal for a man to have intercourse with his wife while he is in itikaf, nor for him to take pleasure in her by kissing her, or whatever. However, I have not heard anyone disapproving of a man, or woman, in itikaf getting married as long as there is no physical relationship. Marriage is not disapproved of for someone fasting.&quot; &quot;There is, however, a distinction between the marriage of someone in itikaf and that of someone who is muhrim, in that some one who is muhrim can eat, drink, visit the sick and attend funerals, but cannot put on perfume, whilst a man or woman in itikaf can put on oil and perfume and groom their hair, but cannot attend funerals or pray over the dead or visit the sick. Thus their situations with regard to marriage are different.&quot; &quot;This is the sunna as it has come down to us regarding marriage for those who are muhrim, doing itikaf, or fasting. Yahya related to me from Malik from Yazid ibn Abdullah ibn al- Hadi from Muhammad ibn Ibrahim al-Harith at-Taymi from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman that Abu Said al-Khudri said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to do itikaf in the middle ten days of Ramadan. One year he was doing itikaf and then, when it came to the night of the twenty-first, which was the night before the morning when he would normally have finished his itikaf, he said, &#x27;Whoever has done i&#x27;tikaf with me should continue doing itikaf for the last ten days. I saw a certain night and then I was made to forget it. I saw myself prostrating the following morning in water and clay. Look for it in the last ten days, and look for it on the odd days.&#x27; &quot; Abu Said continued, &quot;The sky poured with rain that night and the mosque had a roof (made of palm fronds) and the mosque was soaked. With my own eyes I saw the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, leave with traces of water and clay on his forehead and nose, in the morning after the night of the twenty- first</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 697]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/697" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद खुदरी (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) एतिकाफ किया करते थे रमज़ान के दरमियानी अशरे का। तो एक साल एतिकाफ किया, जब 21वीं रात आयी जिस की सुबह को आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) एतिकाफ से बाहर आया करते थे, तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: जिस शख़्स ने मेरे साथ एतिकाफ किया है तो चाहिये और दस दिन तक आखिर में एतिकाफ करे। मैं ने शब-ए-क़द्र को मालूम किया था फिर मैं भुला दिया गया, मैं ख्याल करता हूँ के मैं ने देखा की मैं शब-ए-क़द्र की सुबह को सज्दा करता हूँ कीचड़ और पानी में। पस ढूँढो तुम उसको आखिर दस में से हर ताक़ रात में। अबू सईद खुदरी ने कहा के उसी रात पानी बरसा और मस्जिद की छत पत्तों और शाखों की थी तो टपकी मस्जिद। अबू सईद ने कहा मेरी दोनों आँखों ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को देखा, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) नमाज़ से फ़ारिग हुए और पेशानी और नाक मुबारक पर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के मिट्टी और पानी का निशान था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 697]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/697" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سعید خدری سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اعتکاف کیا کرتے تھے رمضان کے درمیانی عشرے کا تو ایک سال اعتکاف کیا جب اکیسویں رات آئی جس کی صبح کو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اعتکاف سے باہر آیا کرتے تھے تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جس شخص نے میرے ساتھ اعتکاف کیا ہے تو چاہیے اور دس دن تک اخیر میں اعتکاف کرے میں نے شب قدر کو معلوم کیا تھا پھر میں بھلا دیا گیا میں خیال کرتا ہوں کہ میں نے دیکھا کہ میں شب قدر کی صبح کو سجدہ کرتا ہوں کیچڑ اور پانی میں پس ڈھونڈو تم اس کو اخیر دس میں سے ہر طاق رات میں ابوسعید خدری نے کہا کہ اسی رات پانی برسا اور مسجد کی چھت پتوں اور شاخوں کی تھی تو ٹپکی مسجد ابوسعید نے کہا میری دونوں آنکھوں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو دیکھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نماز سے فارغ ہوئے اور پیشانی اور ناک مبارک پر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے مٹی اور پانی کا نشان تھا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 697]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/697" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aitikaf kiya karte the Ramazan ke darmiyani ashre ka. To ek saal aitikaf kiya, jab 21vi raat aayi jis ki subah ko aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aitikaf se bahar aaya karte the, to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jis shakhs ne mere sath aitikaf kiya hai to chahiye aur das din tak akhir mein aitikaf kare. Main ne Shab-e-Qadr ko maloom kiya tha phir main bhula diya gaya, main khayal karta hoon ke main ne dekha ki main Shab-e-Qadr ki subah ko sajda karta hoon kichad aur pani mein. Pas dhundho tum usko akhir das mein se har taaq raat mein. Abu Saeed Khudri ne kaha ke usi raat pani barsa aur masjid ki chhat patton aur shakhon ki thi to tapki masjid. Abu Saeed ne kaha meri dono aankhon ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko dekha, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) namaz se farigh hue aur peshani aur naak mubarak par aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke mitti aur pani ka nishan tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 697]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/697" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
