## Narration 695

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A698)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ تَحَرَّوْا لَيْلَةَ الْقَدْرِ فِي الْعَشْرِ الأَوَاخِرِ مِنْ رَمَضَانَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 698]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/698" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ziyad related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Search for Laylat al-Qadr in the last ten days of Ramadan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 698]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उरवह बिन ज़ुबैर से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ढूंढो तुम शब्-इ-क़द्र को रमज़ान की आखिर दस रातों में.</p>

## Narration 696

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A699)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ تَحَرَّوْا لَيْلَةَ الْقَدْرِ فِي السَّبْعِ الأَوَاخِرِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 699]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/699" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ziyad related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Dinar from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said. &quot;Search for Laylat al-Qadr in the last seven days</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 699]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/699" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 699]</p>

## Narration 697

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A700)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ أُنَيْسٍ الْجُهَنِيَّ، قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنِّي رَجُلٌ شَاسِعُ الدَّارِ فَمُرْنِي لَيْلَةً أَنْزِلُ لَهَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ انْزِلْ لَيْلَةَ ثَلاَثٍ وَعِشْرِينَ مِنْ رَمَضَانَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 700]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/700" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 700</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   700 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ziyad related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;n Nadr, the mawla of Umar ibn Ubaydullah, that Abdullah ibn Unays al-Juhani said to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &quot;Messenger of Allah, I am a man whose house is a long way away. Tell me one night so that I can stop my journey for it.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Stop on the twenty-third night of Ramadan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 700]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/700" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 700</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   700 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू नसर से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन उनइस जहनि ने कहा रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से: या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मेरा घर दूर है तो एक रात मुक़र्रर कीजिये के उस रात में इस मस्जिद में रहूँ और इबादत करून. फ़रमाया आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने: टेसीसवी (२३वी) शब् को रमज़ान में.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 700]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/700" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 700</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   700 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو النصر سے روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن انیس جہنی نے کہا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم میرا گھر دور ہے تو ایک رات مقرر کیجئے کہ اس رات میں اس مسجد میں رہوں اور عبادت کروں فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے تیسویں شب کو رمضان میں</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 700]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/700" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Nasr se riwayat hai ke Abdullah bin Unais Jahni ne kaha Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se: Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) mera ghar door hai to ek raat muqarrar kijiye ke us raat mein is masjid mein rahoon aur ibadat karoon. Farmaya aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne: Teseesvi (23vi) shab ko Ramazan mein.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 700]</p>

## Narration 699

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A702)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رِجَالاً، مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أُرُوا لَيْلَةَ الْقَدْرِ فِي الْمَنَامِ فِي السَّبْعِ الأَوَاخِرِ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ إِنِّي أَرَى رُؤْيَاكُمْ قَدْ تَوَاطَأَتْ فِي السَّبْعِ الأَوَاخِرِ فَمَنْ كَانَ مُتَحَرِّيَهَا فَلْيَتَحَرَّهَا فِي السَّبْعِ الأَوَاخِرِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 702]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/702" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 702</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   702 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ziyad related to me from Malik from Nafi from Ibn &#x27;Umar that some of the companions of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, were shown Laylat al-Qadr in their sleep during the last seven days. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;I see that your visions agree about the last seven days, so whoever is searching for it should do so in the last seven days</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 702]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/702" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ियाद ने मुझ से मालिक से, नाफ़े से, इब्न-ए-उमर (र.अ) से रिवायत की के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के कुछ सहाबा को ख्वाब में लयलतुल-क़द्र आख़िरी सात दिनों में दिखायी गयी। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: मैं देखता हूँ के तुम्हारे ख्वाब आख़िरी सात दिनों के बारे में मुत्तफ़िक हैं, तो जो इसे तलाश कर रहा हो उसे चाहिये के आख़िरी सात दिनों में करे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 702]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/702" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زیاد نے مجھ سے مالک سے، نافع سے، ابن عمر رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے کچھ صحابہ کو خواب میں لیلۃ القدر آخری سات دنوں میں دکھائی گئی۔ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: میں دیکھتا ہوں کہ تمہارے خواب آخری سات دنوں کے بارے میں متفق ہیں، تو جو اسے تلاش کر رہا ہو اسے چاہیے کہ آخری سات دنوں میں کرے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 702]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/702" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ziyad ne mujh se Malik se, Nafi se, Ibn-e-Umar (R.A) se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke kuch sahaba ko khwab mein Laylat al-Qadr akhri saat dino mein dikhayi gayi. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Main dekhta hoon ke tumhare khwab akhri saat dino ke bare mein muttafiq hain, toh jo ise talash kar raha ho use chahiye ke akhri saat dino mein kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 702]</p>

## Narration 700

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A703)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 19</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي زِيَادٌ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ مَنْ، يَثِقُ بِهِ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ يَقُولُ إِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أُرِيَ أَعْمَارَ النَّاسِ قَبْلَهُ أَوْ مَا شَاءَ اللَّهُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَكَأَنَّهُ تَقَاصَرَ أَعْمَارَ أُمَّتِهِ أَنْ لاَ يَبْلُغُوا مِنَ الْعَمَلِ مِثْلَ الَّذِي بَلَغَ غَيْرُهُمْ فِي طُولِ الْعُمْرِ فَأَعْطَاهُ اللَّهُ لَيْلَةَ الْقَدْرِ خَيْرٌ مِنْ أَلْفِ شَهْرٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 703]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/703" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ziyad related to me from Malik that he had heard a man he trusted of the people of knowledge say, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was shown the lifespans of the people (who had gone) before him, or what Allah willed of that, and it was as if the lives of the people of his community had become too short for them to be able to do as many good actions as others before them had been able to do with their long lives, so Allah gave him Laylat al- Qadr, which is better than a thousand months</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 703]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/703" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक को पहुंचा के चाँद सहाबा ने शब्-इ-क़द्र को देखा ख्वाब में रमजान की आखिर सात रातों में रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मैं देखता हूँ के ख्वाब तुम्हारा मुवाफ़िक़ हुआ मेरे ख्वाब के रमजान की आखिर सात रातों में. सो जो कोई तुम में से शब्-इ-क़द्र को ढूंढना चाहें तो ढूंढे अखिर की सात रातों में. इमाम मालिक से रिवायत है के उन्होंने सुना एक मोतबर शख्स से अहल-इ-इल्म में से को अगले लोगों की उम्रें बतलाई गयी जितना अल्लाह को मंज़ूर था उम्रों को कम समझा और ख्याल किया कि ये लोग उनके बराबर अमल न कर सकेंगे को अल्लाह त&#x27;आला ने शब्-इ-क़द्र जो बेहतर है हज़ार महीने से. इमाम मालिक को पहुंचा सईद बिन मुसय्यब कहते थे जो शख्स हाज़िर हो ईशा की जमात में शब्-इ-क़द्र को तो उसने सवाब शब्-इ-क़द्र का हासिल कर लिया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 703]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/703" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک کو پہنچا کہ چند صحابہ نے شب قدر کو دیکھا خواب میں رمضان کی اخیر سات راتوں میں تو فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے میں دیکھتا ہوں کہ خواب تمہارا موافق ہوا میرے خواب کے رمضان کی اخیر سات راتوں میں سو جو کوئی تم میں سے شب قدر کو ڈھونڈنا چاہے تو ڈھونڈے اخیر کی سات راتوں میں ۔ امام مالک سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے سنا ایک معتبر شخص سے اہل علم میں سے کہتے تھے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو اگلے لوگوں کی عمریں بتلائیں گئیں جتنا اللہ کو منظور تھا تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنی امت کی عمروں کو کم سمجھا اور خیال کیا کہ یہ لوگ ان کے برابر عمل نہ کر سکیں گے پس دی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو اللہ تعالیٰ نے شب قدر جو بہتر ہے ہزار مہینے سے ۔ امام مالک کو پہنچا سعید بن مسیب کہتے تھے جو شخص حاضر ہو عشاء کی جماعت میں شب قدر کو تو اس نے ثواب شب قدر کا حاصل کر لیا</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 703]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/703" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ko pahuncha ke chand sahaba ne Shab-e-Qadr ko dekha khwab mein Ramzan ki akhir saat raaton mein, to farmaya Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne main dekhta hoon ke khwab tumhara muwafiq hua mere khwab ke Ramzan ki akhir saat raaton mein. So jo koi tum mein se Shab-e-Qadr ko dhoondna chahe to dhoonde akhir ki saat raaton mein. Imam Malik se riwayat hai ke unhone suna ek motabar shakhs se ahl-e-ilm mein se, kehte the Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko agle logon ki umrein batlayi gayi jitna Allah ko manzoor tha, to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apni ummat ki umron ko kam samjha aur khayal kiya ke ye log unke barabar amal na kar sakenge, pas di aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne Shab-e-Qadr jo behtar hai hazaar mahine se. Imam Malik ko pahuncha Saeed bin Musayyib kehte the jo shakhs hazir ho Isha ki jamaat mein Shab-e-Qadr ko to usne sawab Shab-e-Qadr ka hasil kar liya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 703]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/703" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
